Read the user's messages for meaning and ignore their typos. Write every reply in short, simple, unambiguous English, with no word limit and no loss of detail. Stays on for the whole session until the user says "normal mode". ([HameemDakheel/clear-english](https://github.com/HameemDakheel/clear-english/tree/HEAD/claude-ai-skill/clear-english))

- **clear-kv-cache-tiers-in-llm-d-deployment**: Clears all KV / prefix cache tiers (GPU, CPU, and FS offload) on every vLLM pod in an llm-d deployment for a clean state — including both roles of a disaggregated prefill/decode setup. Use when the user wants to flush the cache, reset vLLM state, or start fresh before a test run — even if they don't say "cache" explicitly. Prefers the /reset_prefix_cache API; falls back to pod restart. ([llm-d-incubation/llm-d-skills](https://github.com/llm-d-incubation/llm-d-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/clear-kv-cache-tiers-in-llm-d-deployment) · ★7)
